## Best date plans

### 2026-11-28 — Beginners + Lightscape Lodge at Yellow Magnolia Cafe

**Beginners, then a meal.** Seasonal American at Lightscape Lodge at Yellow Magnolia Cafe. It is an 18-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 12:15 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=188+Prospect+Park+West&destination=Brooklyn+Botanic+Garden%2C+enter+at+990+Washington+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11225&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://nitehawkcinema.com/prospectpark/movies/beginners/) · [Event booking](https://nitehawkcinema.com/prospectpark/purchase/24875721/) · [Restaurant](https://www.bbg.org/feature/lightscape/lightscape_lodge_dinners) · [Reservations](https://resy.com/cities/new-york-ny/venues/yellow-magnolia-cafe)

### 2026-11-28 — Found Sound Nation: Mara Rosenbloom + Selendis S.A. Johnson + Lightscape Lodge at Yellow Magnolia Cafe

**Found Sound Nation: Mara Rosenbloom + Selendis S.A. Johnson, then dinner.** Seasonal American at Lightscape Lodge at Yellow Magnolia Cafe. It is a 20-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 4 pm, so eat afterwards.

### 2026-11-27 — Grand Central Terminal Architecture Tour + Altbau Bakery

**Grand Central Terminal Architecture Tour, then a meal.** Sourdough bread and pastries at Altbau Bakery. It is 2.6 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 12:30 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=89+East+42nd+Street%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10017&destination=184+North+8th+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11211&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.mas.org/events/grand-central-terminal-11-27-2026/) · [Event booking](https://secure.mas.org/forms/grand-central-terminal-registration-27) · [Restaurant](https://altbaubakery.com/)

### 2026-11-26 — NYC Resistor Public Craft Night + Briscola Trattoria

**Rizzoli Holiday Book Fair — author signings, then dinner.** Sourdough bread and pastries at Altbau Bakery. It is 2.5 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 6:30 pm, so eat afterwards.

**Dinner, then Music Mondays: Ruckus with Keir GoGwilt and Fiona Gillespie.** Italian at Cafe Fiorello. It is 1.5 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Music Mondays: Ruckus with Keir GoGwilt and Fiona Gillespie starts at 7:30 pm, so book the table early.

#### [threeASFOUR: Full Circle — final day](https://filmforum.org/film/threeasfour-full-circle)

6:20 PM · film · $18 general; $12 members · Film Forum · South Village · Manhattan · Score 61.0  
Sean Ono Lennon and Brian C. González document the experimental New York fashion collective threeASFOUR and its struggle to sustain an independent creative practice.
**Timing:** 95-minute film.
**Practical note:** English, German and Arabic with English subtitles.
**Why now:** Last day of the announced engagement.
**Book soon:** Film Forum explicitly lists November 26 as the final day.
[Official](https://filmforum.org/film/threeasfour-full-circle) · [Tickets / booking](https://my.filmforum.org/events/threeasfour-full-circle)

#### [NYC Resistor Public Craft Night](https://www.nycresistor.com/)

6:30 PM · workshop · Free · NYC Resistor · Gowanus · Brooklyn · Score 59.0  
Open craft night at the NYC Resistor hacker collective: bring a project, use the space, meet members. Free and open to the public, 18 and over.
**Timing:** Mondays and Thursdays 6:30-9:30pm. Free, but 18+ only.
**Practical note:** Fourth-floor walkup; confirm on Eventbrite since craft night is occasionally cancelled for private classes.
**Why now:** Runs twice a week year-round and is one of the few genuinely free weeknight drop-in activities.
[Official](https://www.nycresistor.com/) · [Tickets / booking](https://www.eventbrite.com/e/public-craft-night-mondays-and-thursdays-tickets-162729866411)

## Where to eat

Thanksgiving meals, ongoing dining deals, recent openings and upcoming holiday treats for November 26, 2026. Regular promotions are not confirmed for Thanksgiving unless explicitly stated; live availability has not been checked.

Review used the supplied captures and 12 direct public-web checks; two linked PDFs returned unreadable binary content.

### Le Gratin's two- or three-course weekday lunch

Deals & happy hours — Le Gratin

A downtown set lunch with choices including mushroom rigatoni, sausage with lentils and chicken Caesar.

Price: $45 per person for two courses; $55 for three courses.  
Schedule: Monday-Friday, 11:30am-2:30pm.  
Conditions: Supplements: steak tartare starter +$5, skate +$3, cheeseburger +$5 and steak frites +$8. Tax, gratuity and mandatory-reservation terms are not stated. A separate Thanksgiving menu is published; applicability of this regular lunch offer on November 26 remains unconfirmed.  
[Source](https://legratinnyc.com/our-menu/lunch)

### Le Gratin's bar-only $30 frites entrées and $8 mini martinis

Deals & happy hours — Le Gratin

#### [Art is for the Spirit: Records of the Self — closing day](https://www.paulacoopergallery.com/exhibitions/art-is-for-the-spirit)

2026-12-12 · Time to confirm · art · Paula Cooper Gallery · Chelsea · Manhattan · Score 67.0  
Ivy Shapiro organizes works by Jennifer Bartlett, Jonathan Borofsky, Robert Gober, David Hammons, Kazuko Miyamoto, Ree Morton, Elizabeth Murray, and Joel Shapiro around home, autobiography, and intertwined artistic lives.
**Timing:** Exhibition closes December 12. Regular gallery hours Tuesday–Saturday, 10am–6pm.
**Why now:** The show ends in just over two weeks, earlier than the gallery's neighboring Oldenburg exhibition.
**Book soon:** The exhibition-specific official page confirms December 12 as the closing date.

#### [IMAGES: A Revelation — final performance](https://www.amant.org/programs/3132-images-a-revelation-by-gideon-jacobs)

2026-12-10 · 7:30 PM · theater · Free · Amant, Géza · East Williamsburg · Brooklyn · Score 64.0  
Gideon Jacobs performs a fictional preacher obsessed with destroying images in an Amant commission directed by Alex Tatarsky, with Arthur Sillers and Emily Allan. The work includes an installation and theatrical sermons.
**Timing:** Performances December 8–10 at 7:30pm; approximately 50 minutes.
**Practical note:** This is a theatrical performance, not a religious service. Official registration is available through Eventbrite.
**Why now:** The commission ends two weeks from today; the deadline is the final performance, not presumed ticket scarcity.
**Book soon:** Amant confirms only three performance dates, December 8–10, with the final performance at 7:30pm on December 10.

#### [Elektra: newly extended final week](https://tfana.org/events/elektra-2026-11-27-730-pm)

2026-12-08 · 7:30 PM · theater · Regular tickets $70–$98 plus $7 handling; premium $125 plus handling. · Polonsky Shakespeare Center · Fort Greene · Brooklyn · Score 54.0  
Daniel Fish directs Sophokles in Anne Carson's translation, with choral music by Ted Hearne. Newly extended performances offer alternatives to the sold-out or nearly sold-out earlier dates.
**Timing:** Extension runs December 8–13; final performance December 13 at 7:30pm. Running time 80 minutes.
**Practical note:** No late seating; production includes bright lights. Regular tickets fit the $120 budget; premium tickets do not.
**Why now:** A newly opened extension provides a concrete booking opportunity before the December 13 ending.
**Book soon:** The official calendar marks December 8–13 as newly extended. December 3 and the December 6 matinee are sold out, with several surrounding performances showing limited to no availability.

#### [The Lost World: The Art of Minnie Evans — closing day](https://whitney.org/exhibitions/the-lost-world)

2027-01-10 · Time to confirm · museums · Free on the closing day's Second Sunday; regular adult admission otherwise $30. · Whitney Museum of American Art · Meatpacking District · Manhattan · Score 69.0  
A retrospective of Minnie Evans's dream-inspired drawings, paintings, and collages combines intricate patterns, watchful faces, mythical creatures, and imagery from her Afro-Caribbean heritage and North Carolina surroundings.
**Timing:** Closes January 10; regular Sunday museum hours 10:30am–6pm.
**Practical note:** The closing date falls on the second Sunday, covered by the museum's standing free-admission policy. Advance tickets are recommended.
**Why now:** Save the confirmed closing date now; there is no verified immediate sellout pressure.
**On-sale watch:** The exhibition-specific page confirms January 10, 2027 as its final day.
[Official](https://whitney.org/exhibitions/the-lost-world) · [Tickets / booking](https://whitney.org/admission)
**Sale/deadline:** 2027-01-10

#### [Here There Are Blueberries: Broadway closing](https://www.herethereareblueberries.com/)

2027-01-10 · Time to confirm · theater · Ethel Barrymore Theatre · Midtown West · Manhattan · Score 64.0  
Tectonic Theater Project's play follows the investigation of a Nazi-era photograph album received by the U.S. Holocaust Memorial Museum. Conceived and directed by Moisés Kaufman, it is written by Kaufman and Amanda Gronich.
**Timing:** January 10 closing verified; final performance time was not verified.
**Practical note:** Official site identifies Friday and Saturday evenings and December 23–30 as best availability. Telecharge returned HTTP 403, preventing price and inventory verification.
**Why now:** The announced ending creates a planning deadline without claiming that available performances are selling out.
**On-sale watch:** The production's official site explicitly advertises Broadway performances only through January 10.

#### [Claes Oldenburg: Six Themes — closing day](https://www.paulacoopergallery.com/exhibitions/claes-oldenburg)

2026-12-19 · Time to confirm · art · Paula Cooper Gallery · Chelsea · Manhattan · Score 63.0  
Sculptures, drawings, and prints revisit six subjects from Oldenburg's landmark 1975 survey, including the clothespin, geometric mouse, and typewriter eraser. The gallery describes it as the first exhibition focused on this body of work in fifty years.
**Timing:** Exhibition closes December 19. Regular gallery hours Tuesday–Saturday, 10am–6pm.
**Why now:** The rarely assembled survey ends before Christmas.
**On-sale watch:** The official gallery page confirms December 19 as the last day of this loan-based presentation.
